"""Apply the seat's Q2 ruling (A4, tests 1-7; results/raw/2026-10-15_m-design-to-code_step15b_answers.md) to the
table of record's candidate rows (rows_of_record.v2.json, sha256 15ca089b...f420). Revision r5 (the A4 sweep's values-check
passes 1-4 disposed; r3 sha256 6459f870...6de2, r4 cf74a46a...0a92). Writes two rows files:
  rows_of_record.v3.json       -- the sweep: the seat's ten applied cell changes, plus e2b on the five rows the seat
                                  lists under test 5 whose cells lacked it (25.4, 232.1, 232.2, 232.3, 240.6), by test
                                  5's letter ("asserts its null on e2b and the base row too"), where the applied
                                  paragraph names no e2b change. The five are count- and clause-invariant; they change
                                  those rows' (a) cell lists (which cells a posting rewrite must clear);
  rows_seat_enumeration.json   -- the seat's ten applied cell changes only: the five rows keep their cells, with
                                  rationales stating that the variant follows the applied paragraph against test 5's
                                  letter (the option the seat can choose by a strike). Outside the five rows its
                                  rationales are the sweep's.
The ruling decides e2b and olmo-base membership only; no other cell moves (S1 stays on 254.2). Only null_cells,
rationale_by and rationale move; the builder reads neither rationale nor boundary.
usage: python3 -I -B apply_a4.py <rows_v2.json> <outdir>"""
import copy, hashlib, json, sys
from pathlib import Path

def amend(r, new_cells, test, text, label="the A4 sweep"):
    r["null_cells"] = new_cells
    r["rationale_by"] = f"{r['rationale_by']}, as amended by Code ({label}, {test})"
    r["rationale"] = text

def main():
    src, out = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
    base = json.loads(src.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    units = {r["unit"] for r in base}
    groups = (SEAT, SWEEP, REGROUND, APPEND)
    for d in groups + (VARIANT,):
        assert set(d) <= units, set(d) - units
    assert set(VARIANT) == set(SWEEP)
    assert all(not (set(a) & set(b)) for i, a in enumerate(groups) for b in groups[i + 1:])
    for name, mode in (("rows_of_record.v3.json", "sweep"), ("rows_seat_enumeration.json", "variant")):
        rows = copy.deepcopy(base); log = []
        for r in rows:
            u, old = r["unit"], list(r["null_cells"])
            if u in SEAT:
                amend(r, *SEAT[u])
            elif u in SWEEP and mode == "sweep":
                amend(r, *SWEEP[u])
            elif u in SWEEP:
                test, text = VARIANT[u]; amend(r, old, test, text, "the seat-enumeration variant")
            elif u in REGROUND:
                test, text = REGROUND[u]; amend(r, old, test, text)
            elif u in APPEND:
                test, text = APPEND[u]; amend(r, old, test, r["rationale"] + " " + text)
            else:
                continue
            log.append(f"{u}: {','.join(old)} -> {','.join(r['null_cells'])} ({r['rationale_by'].rsplit('(', 1)[-1]}"
                       if r["null_cells"] != old else f"{u}: cells unchanged; rationale ({r['rationale_by'].rsplit('(', 1)[-1]}")
        p = out / name
        p.write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"== {name}: {len(rows)} rows sha256 {hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}")
        print("\n".join("   " + l for l in log))

main()

---- THIS RAW'S GENERATOR (gen_raw.py), verbatim; sha256 18c35c965a401ad575fd44b3758aa327c2fe119086512f8d960a9497708cb6c4. Passes 3 and 4 cite lines of earlier versions
     (pass 3's critic, e.g. 'gen_raw.py:80-82'; pass 4, e.g. ':42' and ':174'), which do not survive; pass 5 read the

"""Assemble the A4 sweep's values-check raw from the scratch files. It asserts the hash of every file in the scratch
directory or the repo that its header states (the r1/r2 output hashes and the commit dd097ab are stated, not asserted), the
extractions' byte-equality with their committed sections, the rows' cell invariance r3 -> r4 -> r5 in both files, the
(a)-count distributions and the 14-row set from both reports, the all-PASS figures from readings2.py's stdout, and the
segmenter output's size; the header says which figures rest on the checkers' loops instead.
usage: python3 -I -B gen_raw.py <scratch_a4_dir> <repo> <wf_scripts_dir> <out.txt> <stamp>"""
import hashlib, json, re, sys
from pathlib import Path

A, REPO, WF, OUT, STAMP = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3]), Path(sys.argv[4]), sys.argv[5]
sha = lambda p: hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
txt = lambda p: Path(p).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
def verbatim(p):
    t = txt(p)
    return (t, "") if t.endswith("\n") else (t + "\n", " (a trailing newline added)")

    assert sha(p) == h, (p, sha(p))

                            ("build", A / "build_table.py"), ("gen", Path(__file__)))}

        "var": [load(A / "rows_seat_enumeration.r3.json"), load(A / "rows_seat_enumeration.r4.json")]}

assert all("e2b" in a["null_cells"] and "e2b" not in b["null_cells"]
           and set(a["null_cells"]) - set(b["null_cells"]) == {"e2b"} for a, b in zip(v3, var) if a["unit"] in FIVE)
for k, cur in (("v3", v3), ("var", var)):                       # r3 -> r4 -> r5 moves no cell, no non-rationale field
    for old in prev[k]:
        assert all({x: y for x, y in a.items() if x != "rationale"} == {x: y for x, y in b.items() if x != "rationale"}
                   for a, b in zip(old, cur)), k
touched = [b["unit"] for a, b in zip(v2, v3) if a != b]
changed_since_r3 = sorted({b["unit"] for k, cur in (("v3", v3), ("var", var)) for old in prev[k]
                           for a, b in zip(old, cur) if a != b} |
                          {b["unit"] for k in prev for a, b in zip(prev[k][0], prev[k][1]) if a != b},
                          key=lambda u: [r["unit"] for r in v3].index(u))

def rep(p):
    lines = txt(p).splitlines(); assert lines[0] == "table_problems: []", (p, lines[0])
    c = {}
    for line in lines:
        if line.startswith("R"):
            f = line.split("\t"); c.setdefault(int(f[5].split()[0]), []).append(f[0])
    return c

for r in v3:

        o2 = v2d[r["unit"]]
        cc = ("cells unchanged" if o2["null_cells"] == r["null_cells"]
              else f"{','.join(o2['null_cells'])} -> {','.join(r['null_cells'])}")
        w(f"[{r['unit']}] {cc}; boundary {r['boundary']}\n  v2 rationale_by: {o2['rationale_by']}\n  v2 rationale: {o2['rationale']}\n")

  f" the r5 texts are in (1) and (2))\n")

"""Assemble the A4 sweep's values-check raw from the scratch files. It asserts every hash its header states, the
extractions' byte-equality with their committed sections, the rows' cell invariance r3 -> r4 -> r5 in both files, the
(a)-count distributions and the 14-row set from both reports, the all-PASS figures from readings2.py's stdout, and the
segmenter output's size; the header says which figures rest on the checkers' loops instead.
usage: python3 -I -B gen_raw.py <scratch_a4_dir> <repo> <wf_scripts_dir> <out.txt> <stamp>"""
import hashlib, json, re, sys
from pathlib import Path

A, REPO, WF, OUT, STAMP = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2]), Path(sys.argv[3]), Path(sys.argv[4]), sys.argv[5]
sha = lambda p: hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
txt = lambda p: Path(p).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
def verbatim(p):
    t = txt(p)
    return (t, "") if t.endswith("\n") else (t + "\n", " (a trailing newline added)")

WANT = {
    REPO / "PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md": "45b67246f2b2bcb40774e95462ad4e3c5f559fdd0358387cceec0155bebe7a41",
    REPO / "scripts/m_analyze.py": "655e0f7f6743d0bf497f5ba97ce37f7febf9fcf6a45e068b5385ee945c5dc16f",
    REPO / "valence_core/m/resume.py": "bec6358a9c4e5e49f77227f7dcc8d698d2c780cda50e27a8dea90d39e667fb1a",
    REPO / "results/raw/2026-10-15_m-design-to-code_step15b_answers.md": "db9edf3be03a157558e29d25c45c620c9db28f8837b106981fb8389336619a7b",

def rep(p):
    lines = txt(p).splitlines(); assert lines[0] == "table_problems: []", (p, lines[0])
    c = {}
    for line in lines:
        if line.startswith("R"):
            f = line.split("\t"); c.setdefault(int(f[5].split()[0]), []).append(f[0])
    return c

====================================================================================================================
(4) THE A4 SWEEP'S PASS 1 (on r1): two checkers, rows and mechanics
====================================================================================================================
==== PASS 1, CHECKER "rows" (general-purpose agent), prompt verbatim
You are a STRICT, adversarial values-checker. Find errors; do not rubber-stamp. Do not modify any file under /home/user/jspace-lab (read-only). Work only in /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-jspace-lab/bb999a06-1639-56eb-8688-cc04a3615696/scratchpad/a4/vc_rows/ (create it). Do NOT open anything under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/.

Context. A design seat issued a ruling ("A4", tests 1-7) on which cells a sentence ("unit") of a preprint asserts a null on. Read the ruling in full: /home/user/jspace-lab/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-design-to-code_step15b_answers.md (section "Q2 — the ruling (A4)"). The classification spec is /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-jspace-lab/bb999a06-1639-56eb-8688-cc04a3615696/scratchpad/a4/spec.md. The preprint is /home/user/jspace-lab/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md; unit "L.S" = line L, sentence S as segmented in scratchpad/a4/sentences_v8.json (fields line, sentence, section, text). The ruling's tests decide only whether a unit's null covers e2b or olmo-base (the "general list" is e2a, f1, olmo-sft, olmo-dpo, olmo-instruct).

Inputs: scratchpad/a4/rows_v2.json (the candidate before the ruling), scratchpad/a4/rows_of_record.v3.json (Code's sweep), scratchpad/a4/rows_seat_enumeration.json (only the seat's own enumerated changes), scratchpad/a4/apply_a4.py (the script that made them).

Code's sweep = the seat's "Applied to the candidate" changes PLUS six firing-invariant e2b corrections Code derived: +e2b on 25.4, 232.1, 232.2, 232.3, 240.6 (test 5: "data-wide and lineage-wide words reach what they cover"; "two families"/"these lineages" cover Qwen, hence e2b), and -e2b on 262.5 (test 3 says its "self-administration nulls" names e2a; "at each stage" of the ontogeny reaches base, not e2b). Code also dropped S1 from 254.2 under test 3's "take the general list".

Tasks:
1. For EVERY one of the 55 rows in rows_of_record.v3.json, read the unit's full text (and surrounding paragraph in the preprint where tests 6/7 need context) and decide independently whether e2b and olmo-base belong under tests 1-7, in order. Report every row where your answer differs from v3, with the test and quoted text.
2. Check that v3 implements every change the seat's "Applied to the candidate" paragraph names, and nothing the seat forbids (e.g. 234.4 must not move with 236.2; 240.3 keeps base; 186.2 trades e2b for base; the five excluded units stay out).
3. Judge Code's six extra e2b corrections and the S1 drop: is each the better reading of the ruling's text, or does the seat's own enumeration (which omits them) better reflect the ruling? Argue both sides briefly, then give a verdict per row.

Return findings with severity fails (a value, cell, hash, count or quote is wrong, or a rationale contradicts its row or the ruling), imprecise (true but misstated or incomplete), or note. Quote evidence. Be concise; if you find nothing, return an empty list and say what you checked.`

const FINDINGS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    checked: { type: 'string', description: 'what you checked, in two or three sentences' },
    findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, row: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
      finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'row', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
    figures: { type: 'string', description: 'mechanics lens only: the figures from (iii); empty otherwise' },
  },
  required: ['checked', 'findings', 'figures'],
}
const VERDICTS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['confirmed', 'confirmed-with-correction', 'refuted'] },
      severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, corrected_fix: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'corrected_fix'] } },
    missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' }, description: 'errors in the lens scope the lens missed' },
  },
  required: ['verdicts', 'missed'],
}

const results = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYOUR LENS (key ${l.key}): ${l.task}`, { label: `lens:${l.key}`, phase: 'Lens', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (lens, l) => lens && agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the STRICT REFUTER for lens ${l.key}, whose task was: ${l.task}\n\nIts findings (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(lens.findings)}\n\nFor EACH finding, independently reproduce it from the files and try to REFUTE it; default to refuted only when the evidence fails on your own check. Correct its severity and fix where needed. Then re-run the lens's scope yourself, briefly, and list any error it missed.`,
    { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS }).then(v => ({ key: l.key, lens, refuter: v })),
)

const done = results.filter(Boolean)
const critic = await agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the COMPLETENESS CRITIC. Four lenses and their strict refuters checked the A4 sweep r2. Their outputs (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(done)}\n\nAsk what is missing: a row, unit or cited line nobody opened; a claim in apply_a4.py's docstring or the rationales nobody verified; a figure the RESULTS entry will state that nobody reproduced (the 14-row set, the table shas, the (a)-count distribution, the all-PASS enumeration under both flags, the six rows whose (a) cell lists differ between the variants); a unit outside the 55 rows that the ruling's tests would bring INTO the table (the seat says the five units 238.1, 184.3, 292.4, 184.5, 240.4 stay out — check those and scan the preprint's other units for any test-5 data-wide or lineage-wide null the table lacks). Check what you can yourself and return findings.`,
  { label: 'critic', phase: 'Critic', schema: FINDINGS })

return { lenses: done, critic }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 2 (disp_tvc2.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the A4 sweep's values-check, pass 2 (four lenses, each with a strict refuter; a completeness critic)

Revision r3 disposes pass 2. The sweep is now the seat's ten applied cell changes plus e2b on the five rows the seat
lists under test 5 whose cells lacked it (25.4, 232.1, 232.2, 232.3, 240.6), by test 5's letter. R262.05 keeps e2b, as
in the seat's applied paragraph (r1-r2 had removed it). Rows c2945f1a…bbd3; table 74781b90…f907; table_problems [].
The seat-only variant: rows e6e186ed…025e; table 29a878cf…5490 (cells unchanged from r2); table_problems []. The
variants differ on exactly those five rows' (a) cell lists; every count, clause and item set is identical; 14 rows fire
(a) in all 1,080 in both.

## Applied (cells)

- **B1, B10, C1, V4, the asymmetry the rows-b refuter and the mechanics refuter name** (CONFIRMED): r1-r2 added e2b
  to five test-5 rows by test 5's letter and removed it from R262.05 against the same letter, by the heading and by
  test 3's precedence; the precedence would also strip R262.05's base row (test 3 lists "the ontogeny nulls") and
  R220.01's cells. r3 applies the letter uniformly to every row the seat lists under test 5: R262.05 keeps e2b, now by
  test 5, not the spec's mapping of "the self-administration nulls" (which test 3 says names e2a). The departure from
  the applied paragraph is the five +e2b rows only.

## Applied (rationales; no cell change)

- **M1, A1** (CONFIRMED): R033.02, R248.02, R276.05 now credit e2b to the assembly (the classification critic's
  challenge) and only olmo-base to the values-check's T3 (interim raw :2452-2454).
- **M2, B9, the mechanics refuter's 254.2 note** (CONFIRMED): R254.02's S1 rests on pass 1's T4 (180.3's kind-1
  label), not pass 2's K5 (a quotation fix); the rationale states that test 3's enumeration omits S1 and that the
  ruling decides e2b and base only, and flags the tension for the seat.
- **A2** (CONFIRMED, note): R168.01 records the seat's "R168.01 keeps 184.4's ground".

  (R186.02, R186.04, R236.02, R240.03) say the row fires (a) in all 1,080 and is listed by the enumeration for rewrite.

const FINDINGS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    checked: { type: 'string' },
    findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, row: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] },
      finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'row', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } },
    figures: { type: 'string' },
  },
  required: ['checked', 'findings', 'figures'],
}
const VERDICTS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['confirmed', 'confirmed-with-correction', 'refuted'] },
      severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, corrected_fix: { type: 'string' } },
      required: ['id', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'corrected_fix'] } },
    missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
  },
  required: ['verdicts', 'missed'],
}

const results = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYOUR LENS (key ${l.key}): ${l.task}`, { label: `lens:${l.key}`, phase: 'Lens', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (lens, l) => lens && agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the STRICT REFUTER for lens ${l.key}, whose task was: ${l.task}\n\nIts findings (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(lens.findings)}\n\nFor EACH finding, reproduce it from the files and try to REFUTE it; refute only when your own check fails it. Correct severity and fix where needed. Then re-run the lens's scope yourself, briefly, and list any error it missed.`,
    { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS }).then(v => ({ key: l.key, lens, refuter: v })),
)
const done = results.filter(Boolean)
const critic = await agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the COMPLETENESS CRITIC for pass 3. The lenses' and refuters' outputs (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(done)}\n\nWhat is missing: a touched rationale nobody read in full; a cell call nobody re-derived under tests 1-7 after r3 (re-derive the 14 rows carrying e2b or olmo-base and the rows the seat names by number yourself); a figure the RESULTS entry will state that nobody reproduced; any inconsistency between the r3 rationales and the ruling's text. Check what you can and return findings.`,
  { label: 'critic', phase: 'Critic', schema: FINDINGS })
return { lenses: done, critic }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 3 (disp_tvc3.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the A4 sweep's values-check, pass 3 (three lenses, each with a strict refuter; a completeness critic; on r3)

Naming: "pass N" below is the A4 sweep's pass N; the interim raw's passes (results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-of-record-
valuescheck_interim.txt) are named as such. disp_tvc1.md and disp_tvc2.md are kept as the passes read them (pass 3's
findings cite their line numbers); their corrections are here.

Revision r4 disposes pass 3. No cell moves: both tables are unchanged (table of record 74781b90…f907; variant
29a878cf…5490; table_problems [] on both). The rows change in rationale text only: table of record rows r3 c2945f1a…bbd3
-> r4 afe755fe…fd67 (7 rows: 194.4, 236.2, 242.1, 248.6, 250.3, 254.2, 262.5); variant rows r3 e6e186ed…025e -> r4
c7062ec4…bbc1 (those 7 and the five variant rows). apply_a4.py r3 sha256 6459f870…6de2 (kept in scratch as
apply_a4.r3.py) -> r4 cf74a46a…0a92. readings2.py (the interim raw's script) run as-is on both r4 files and on v2
confirms the figures the entry states: 14 rows (a)=1080 in both, all-PASS 23 items (flag TRUE) and 24 (FALSE), the
candidate 17, 27 and 28.

  letter governs a listed row", which the variant's five rows set aside. r4 makes it neutral: the listing governs

export const meta = {
  name: 'a4-sweep-valuescheck-pass4',
  description: 'Values-check pass 4 of the A4 sweep (r4) and the raw draft: two lenses, a strict refuter each',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Lens', detail: 'r4 rows and dispositions; the raw draft' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'a strict refuter per lens' },
  ],
}
const S = '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-jspace-lab/bb999a06-1639-56eb-8688-cc04a3615696/scratchpad/a4'
const REPO = '/home/user/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `You are a STRICT, adversarial values-checker. Find errors; do not rubber-stamp. Never modify anything under ${REPO} (read-only). Write only under ${S}/tvc4/<your-key>/ (create it). Never open, list or traverse anything under ${REPO}/data/ (do not run find from the repo root). Run Python ONLY as python3 -I -B.

Context. The seat's ruling A4 (tests 1-7; decides e2b and olmo-base membership only): ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-design-to-code_step15b_answers.md. Preprint ${REPO}/PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (45b67246…7a41); units in ${S}/sentences_v8.json. Code's sweep r4: ${S}/apply_a4.py (sha256 cf74a46a…0a92) makes ${S}/rows_of_record.v3.json (afe755feb0417c0ae92d5fd91a373a892cff7463efff59ebe563d413df6ffd67) and ${S}/rows_seat_enumeration.json (c7062ec40ae14450d1162645a36a5fd96e3f8afdf03cf285d889e593caa0bbc1) from ${S}/rows_v2.json; tables ${S}/tab_rows_of_record.v3.json (74781b9044b6aa4a490d6542f10b16e6a2d02ea7a72d524195b41ab6621df907) and ${S}/tab_rows_seat_enumeration.json (29a878cf578cebf7f90dea1292447f37d208051336294648482abf69222d5490). r3's files: ${S}/apply_a4.r3.py, ${S}/rows_of_record.v3.r3.json, ${S}/rows_seat_enumeration.r3.json. Pass 3's returns: ${S}/tvc3_result.json; Code's dispositions of pass 3: ${S}/disp_tvc3.md (and of passes 1-2: disp_tvc1.md, disp_tvc2.md, kept as those passes read them). The interim raw = ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-of-record-valuescheck_interim.txt. The raw draft to be committed: ${S}/raw_draft.txt, built by ${S}/gen_raw.py (it will be regenerated with one more section, this pass, appended; check everything else).
Severity: fails (a value, cell, hash, count, quote or citation is wrong; a rationale contradicts its row, another row in the same file, or the ruling; a disposition claims a change the files do not carry; a header claim of the raw is false), imprecise, note. Quote evidence. Be concise; an empty list is a fine answer if you checked and found nothing — say what you checked.`
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'rows-r4', task: `(i) Re-run apply_a4.py r4 from rows_v2.json into your dir: byte-identical outputs. (ii) Diff r3 -> r4 in both files: confirm no cell moves and list the rationale changes; for each, check it implements the pass-3 disposition it cites in disp_tvc3.md, and that disp_tvc3.md's every 'applied' claim is in the files. (iii) Read in full every rationale that changed r3 -> r4 (both files) and check every quote byte-exact against its source (preprint/sentences_v8, the ruling, spec ${S}/spec.md, rows_v2, interim raw) and every citation (unit numbers, :N preprint lines, 'interim raw :N'). (iv) Consistency: in EACH file separately, look for any two rationales that contradict each other about what governs a test-5 row, test 3 vs test 5 precedence, S1, or which test a phrase belongs to (the class of pass-3 finding F1). (v) Confirm the pass-3 findings disp_tvc3.md marks refuted or not applied (Q1) are rightly so: K1's option (2) at interim raw :3467.` },
  { key: 'raw', task: `The raw draft ${S}/raw_draft.txt as a committed record. (i) Every claim in its header (lines before the first ==== separator): hashes, counts, figures, the five rows, provenance hashes for r1-r4, the process/disclosure lines, the 'FOR THE SEAT' items: check each against the files and the sections below; flag anything false or unsupported. Be specific about the process line: Code's builds through r2 ran python3 -I (bytecode), pass 2's agents were told '-I (or -s -B)', __pycache__ dirs were removed before r3, and none exists now (check git -C ${REPO} status --short --ignored). (ii) Each section's 'verbatim' content equals its source file byte for byte (the table, rows, reports, apply_a4.py, its stdout, readings2 stdout ${S}/readings_r4.txt, prompts ${S}/tvc1_prompts.txt, returns, dispositions, workflow scripts under /root/.claude/projects/-home-user-jspace-lab/bb999a06-1639-56eb-8688-cc04a3615696/workflows/scripts/). (iii) Read gen_raw.py: are its assertions sufficient for what the header states, and does any header figure escape an assertion? (iv) Would a reader of the raw alone be able to rebuild the table of record from the committed text (builder and segmenter in the table raw; rows here; analyzer and preprint at HEAD)? Try it: extract the rows from section (1) of the draft, rebuild with the committed builder (table raw lines 378-445) and segmenter (328-373), and compare the table hash.` },
]
const FINDINGS = { type: 'object', properties: { checked: { type: 'string' }, findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, row: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'row', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } } }, required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const VERDICTS = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['confirmed', 'confirmed-with-correction', 'refuted'] }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, corrected_fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'corrected_fix'] } }, missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const results = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYOUR LENS (key ${l.key}): ${l.task}`, { label: `lens:${l.key}`, phase: 'Lens', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (lens, l) => lens && agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the STRICT REFUTER for lens ${l.key}, whose task was: ${l.task}\n\nIts findings (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(lens.findings)}\n\nFor EACH finding, reproduce it and try to REFUTE it; refute only when your own check fails it. Correct severity and fix. Then re-run the lens's scope yourself, briefly, and list any error it missed.`,
    { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS }).then(v => ({ key: l.key, lens, refuter: v })),
)
return { lenses: results.filter(Boolean) }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 4 (disp_tvc4.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the A4 sweep's values-check, pass 4 (two lenses, each with a strict refuter; on r4 and the raw's draft)

Revision r5 disposes pass 4. No cell moves; both tables are unchanged (74781b90…f907; 29a878cf…5490). Rows change in
rationale text on R242.01 and R262.05 only, in both files: table of record r4 afe755fe…fd67 -> r5 025dca29…a83f;
variant r4 c7062ec4…bbc1 -> r5 3407b460…e728. apply_a4.py r4 cf74a46a…0a92 (kept in scratch as apply_a4.r4.py) -> r5
8e7a1e87…ba4e. The raw is regenerated from the current generator (whose text is now in the raw) with this pass appended;
the draft pass 4 read (6c4bf11b…50e3) is superseded.

## Applied (rows)

- **R4-8** (note): R242.01 now says the heading is read "as to e2b and the base row" at the scope the per-cell labels
  give it, so it no longer uses that phrase in a second sense beside R194.04, R005.01 and R236.01 (where it means the
  general list).
- **the rows refuter's F5 note** (note): R242.01 was the only touched boundary row with no Boundary sentence (v2 had
  none); it now ends "Boundary (judgement; e2b and the base row settled by test 4)". disp_tvc3.md's F5 line ("R061.03
  and R242.01 keep v2's boundary text") was wrong for R242.01.
- **the rows refuter's order note** (note): R262.05 now acknowledges that the ruling applies its tests "in this order"
  (test 3 before test 5) and names what carries the cells: the seat's explicit listing under test 5, with the applied
  paragraph.

## Applied (the raw)

- **RAW-1, R4-6** (FAILS / note, confirmed): the draft's process line was stale; the raw is regenerated, and its

export const meta = {
  name: 'a4-sweep-valuescheck-pass5',
  description: 'Values-check pass 5 of the A4 sweep (r5) and the regenerated raw: one lens, a strict refuter',
  phases: [ { title: 'Lens', detail: 'the regenerated raw, r5, disp_tvc4' }, { title: 'Refute', detail: 'strict refuter' } ],
}
const S = '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-jspace-lab/bb999a06-1639-56eb-8688-cc04a3615696/scratchpad/a4'
const REPO = '/home/user/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `You are a STRICT, adversarial values-checker. Find errors; do not rubber-stamp. Never modify anything under ${REPO} (read-only). Write only under ${S}/tvc5/<your-key>/ (create it). Never open, list, grep or traverse anything under ${REPO}/data/, and never grep or open ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt (a sealed stage raw): grep only named files, never globs over results/raw/. Do not run find from the repo root. Run Python ONLY as python3 -I -B.

Context: the raw ${S}/raw_draft2.txt is about to be committed as the record of the A4 sweep (the seat's ruling ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-15_m-design-to-code_step15b_answers.md applied to the sentence table). It was generated by ${S}/gen_raw.py (whose text is also section (3) of the raw). Revision r5 of ${S}/apply_a4.py made ${S}/rows_of_record.v3.json and ${S}/rows_seat_enumeration.json; r4's are ${S}/apply_a4.r4.py, ${S}/rows_of_record.v3.r4.json, ${S}/rows_seat_enumeration.r4.json. Pass 4's returns ${S}/tvc4_result.json; Code's dispositions of pass 4 ${S}/disp_tvc4.md. The raw will be regenerated once more with this pass appended as section (8); check everything else.
Severity: fails (a header claim of the raw is false or unsupported; a hash, count, quote or citation is wrong; a disposition claims a change the files do not carry; a rationale contradicts its row, another row in the same file or the ruling), imprecise, note. Quote evidence. Be concise; an empty findings list is a fine answer if you checked — say what you checked.`
const TASK = `(i) Every claim in the raw's header (lines before the first ==== separator), especially the READS and BYTECODE disclosure paragraphs, the FIRING COUNTS paragraph's split between what the generator asserts and what rests on checkers' loops, the provenance item and the FOR THE SEAT items: verify each against the files, gen_raw.py's assertions and the sections below. (ii) Re-run gen_raw.py into your own dir with the same stamp as the draft's header line and confirm the output equals raw_draft2.txt byte for byte except the generator-section sha256 if gen_raw.py differs (it should not). (iii) The r4 -> r5 diff: re-run apply_a4.py into your dir (byte-identical rows), confirm only R242.01 and R262.05 rationales change in both files and no other field, and check the two new texts' quotes byte-exact (the ruling's 'in this order' at its Q2 opening; the rest against rows and preprint) and that they do not contradict any other rationale in the same file. (iv) For every pass-4 finding and refuter 'missed' item in tvc4_result.json, check disp_tvc4.md disposes it and that each 'applied' claim is true of the files or the raw. (v) The superseded-rationales section of the raw: for each row listed, the r3/r4 texts equal the r3/r4 files' rationales, and no row whose rationale changed after r3 in either file is missing (outside the five variant rows the variant's texts are the sweep's).`
const F = { type: 'object', properties: { checked: { type: 'string' }, findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, row: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'row', 'severity', 'finding', 'evidence', 'fix'] } } }, required: ['checked', 'findings'] }
const V = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['confirmed', 'confirmed-with-correction', 'refuted'] }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['fails', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, corrected_fix: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'severity', 'evidence', 'corrected_fix'] } }, missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const lens = await agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYOUR LENS (key raw5): ${TASK}`, { label: 'lens:raw5', phase: 'Lens', schema: F })
const refuter = lens && await agent(`${COMMON}\n\nYou are the STRICT REFUTER for lens raw5, whose task was: ${TASK}\n\nIts findings (JSON): ${JSON.stringify(lens.findings)}\n\nFor EACH finding, reproduce it and try to REFUTE it; refute only when your own check fails it. Correct severity and fix. Then re-run the lens's scope yourself, briefly, and list any error it missed.`, { label: 'refute:raw5', phase: 'Refute', schema: V })
return { lenses: [{ key: 'raw5', lens, refuter }] }

# Code's dispositions of the A4 sweep's values-check, pass 5 (one lens, a strict refuter; on r5 and the raw's second draft)

Pass 5 found nothing in the rows: r5 reproduces byte for byte, the r4 -> r5 change is R242.01's and R262.05's
rationales only, their quotes are byte-exact, they contradict no other rationale in either file, and the lens's own loop
(under an audit hook refusing data/ and the stage raw; it never fired) confirms on r5 every firing figure the header
states. No rows or table change after pass 5. Its findings concern the raw's header, the generator and disp_tvc4.md;
all are applied below, in the header text the regeneration carries. These last edits were checked by Code only
(no pass 6): they change wording of the header and of disp files, no row, table or figure.

  paragraph named two content scans of the stage raw; the session's transcripts show more. It now lists every one

  mechanics lens and dispositions refuter; pass 4's raw lens, rows refuter and raw refuter), says each opened and read

  separately. The claim stands that no line of the stage raw or of any file under data/ was displayed (pass 5 counted
